 522 | static uint64_t bb_getsize_in_512sect(in | True                                                                                                                                                                         | 
 523 | {                                        | True                                                                                                                                                                         | 
 524 |  const char *how;                        | True                                                                                                                                                                         | 
 525 |  uint64_t sz64;                          | True                                                                                                                                                                         | 
 526 |  /* BLKGETSIZE64 takes pointer to uint64 | True                                                                                                                                                                         | 
 527 |  uint64_t sz_get64;                      | True                                                                                                                                                                         | 
 528 |  /* BLKGETSIZE takes pointer to ulong: * | True                                                                                                                                                                         | 
 529 |  unsigned long sectors_long;             | True                                                                                                                                                                         | 
 530 |                                          | True                                                                                                                                                                         | 
 531 |  if (ioctl(fd, BLKGETSIZE, &sectors_long | True                                                                                                                                                                         | 
 532 |  ) {                                     | True                                                                                                                                                                         | 
 533 |   /* If in "fdisk -b 4096 /dev/512byte_s | True                                                                                                                                                                         | 
 534 |    * IOW: sector size is OVERRIDDEN by u | True                                                                                                                                                                         | 
 535 |    * the user tries to create 4K sector  | True                                                                                                                                                                         | 
 536 |    * device (presumably because the devi | True                                                                                                                                                                         | 
 537 |    * in 4K mode on this machine, but can | True                                                                                                                                                                         | 
 538 |    * __do NOT assume BLKGETSIZE returns  | True                                                                                                                                                                         | 
 539 |    * it always returns 512-sized "sector | True                                                                                                                                                                         | 
 540 |    */                                    | True                                                                                                                                                                         | 
 541 |   sz64 = (uint64_t)sectors_long * 512;   | True                                                                                                                                                                         | 
 542 |   how = "BLKGETSIZE";                    | True                                                                                                                                                                         | 
 543 |  } else {                                | True                                                                                                                                                                         | 
 544 |   /* BLKGETSIZE failed, assume this is a | True                                                                                                                                                                         | 
 545 | #if ENABLE_FDISK_SUPPORT_LARGE_DISKS     | True                                                                                                                                                                         | 
 546 |   off64_t sz = lseek64(fd, 0, SEEK_END); | ENABLE_FDISK_SUPPORT_LARGE_DISKS                                                                                                                                             | 
 547 |   if (sz == (off64_t)-1)                 | ENABLE_FDISK_SUPPORT_LARGE_DISKS                                                                                                                                             | 
 548 |    bb_perror_msg_and_die("lseek(%llu, %d | ENABLE_FDISK_SUPPORT_LARGE_DISKS                                                                                                                                             | 
 549 | #else                                    | True                                                                                                                                                                         | 
 550 |   off_t sz = xlseek(fd, 0, SEEK_END);    | !ENABLE_FDISK_SUPPORT_LARGE_DISKS                                                                                                                                            | 
 551 | #endif                                   | True                                                                                                                                                                         | 
 552 |   xlseek(fd, 0, SEEK_SET);               | True                                                                                                                                                                         | 
 553 |   sz64 = sz;                             | True                                                                                                                                                                         | 
 554 |   how = "seek";                          | True                                                                                                                                                                         | 
 555 |  }                                       | True                                                                                                                                                                         | 
 556 |  /* We did the above in case BLKGETSIZE6 | True                                                                                                                                                                         | 
 557 |   * If it is, double-check:              | True                                                                                                                                                                         | 
 558 |   */                                     | True                                                                                                                                                                         | 
 559 |  if (ioctl(fd, BLKGETSIZE64, &sz_get64)  | True                                                                                                                                                                         | 
 560 |   && sz_get64 != sz64                    | True                                                                                                                                                                         | 
 561 |  ) {                                     | True                                                                                                                                                                         | 
 562 |   /* Something is very fishy. Abort. */  | True                                                                                                                                                                         | 
 563 |   bb_error_msg_and_die(                  | True                                                                                                                                                                         | 
 564 |    "bad sizes: BLKGETSIZE64:%llu %s:%llu | True                                                                                                                                                                         | 
 565 |    (ullong)sz_get64,                     | True                                                                                                                                                                         | 
 566 |    how, (ullong)sz64                     | True                                                                                                                                                                         | 
 567 |   );                                     | True                                                                                                                                                                         | 
 568 |  }                                       | True                                                                                                                                                                         | 
 569 | #if 0 /* this breaks "fdisk -s FILE" (ut | True                                                                                                                                                                         | 
 570 |  if ((sz64 & 0x1ff) != 0)                | 0                                                                                                                                                                            | 
 571 |   bb_error_msg("size %llu not a multiple | 0                                                                                                                                                                            | 
 572 | #endif                                   | True                                                                                                                                                                         | 
 573 |                                          | True                                                                                                                                                                         | 
 574 |  /* Got bytes, convert to 512 byte block | True                                                                                                                                                                         | 
 575 |  return sz64 >> 9;                       | True                                                                                                                                                                         | 
 576 | }                                        | True                                                                                                                                                                         | 
 577 |                                          | True                                                                                                                                                                         |
